    "newString": "The decomposition (\\S\\ref{sec:density}) is a preliminary upper-bound estimate on the Qwen family only (CodeLlama and DeepSeek exhibit label-only collapse); $N{=}4$ models is insufficient for a continuous density--accuracy relationship.\nHumanEval/MBPP covers only simple assert-based tests; real-world failure modes remain untested.",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\n\\paragraph{A two-pathway model of verdict signals.}\nThe results suggest execution traces contain two independent signal pathways for verdict prediction, whose interaction explains why complete traces degrade SFT learning.\n\n\\paragraph{Explicit pathway: diagnostic tokens.}\nException and assertion events directly encode fault occurrence.\nThe diagnostic token ratio $d$ (\\S2.1) correlates with accuracy at $r{=}0.949$ ($p{=}0.014$) across five conditions, and all four models achieve ${\\geq}$99.33\\% under \\texttt{exception\\_only} with zero variance on Qwen ($\\sigma{=}$0.00, 3 seeds).\nWe acknowledge that exception events structurally encode verdict information; the contribution is systematic quantification across four models and three families.\nAlthough 55.5\\% of failures are silent logic errors, \\texttt{exception\\_only} achieves 100\\% fail recall because test assertions generate \\texttt{AssertionError} events, confirming the pathway captures both crashes and assertion failures.\n\\texttt{ae\\_only}, a strict superset, slightly underperforms \\texttt{exception\\_only} (Table~\\ref{tab:ablation}), confirming that additional content introduces noise.\n\n\\paragraph{Implicit pathway: terminal execution state.}\nTail-truncated traces achieve 93--97\\% accuracy (Table~\\ref{tab:tail_cross_model}) despite containing near-zero diagnostic tokens ($d {\\approx} 0$).\nThe mechanism: whole-input tail truncation at ${\\geq}$512 tokens captures \\texttt{[Line~X]} variable values at final execution steps, which indirectly encode program outcome.\nBelow 384 tokens, the globals module dump consumes the entire budget, yielding ${\\sim}$73\\% ($\\approx$ \\texttt{no\\_trace}).\nThe +13.8pp phase transition at tail-384$\\to$tail-512 marks where the model crosses this noise floor and accesses terminal execution state.\nThis pathway is genuinely non-obvious: it operates on structured program state with zero explicit fault markers.\n\n\\paragraph{Noise dilution in complete traces.}\nBoth pathways' signals exist in full traces but are buried under thousands of intermediate tokens.\nFigure~\\ref{fig:training_dynamics} confirms: \\texttt{exception\\_only} converges within 50 steps, while \\texttt{full\\_trace} with label-only loss oscillates (82--89\\%) without convergence (Appendix~\\ref{sec:training_dynamics}); seed variance negatively correlates with diagnostic density.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{training_dynamics}\n\\caption{Training dynamics: \\texttt{exception\\_only} converges to 99.83\\% by step 150; \\texttt{full\\_trace} with label-only loss oscillates (82--89\\%) without convergence.}\n\\label{fig:training_dynamics}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{Why SFT over regex?}\nSFT \\texttt{exception\\_only} (99.83\\%) exceeds the best regex (B\\_marker, 99.33\\%) by 0.50pp on 6 caught-exception boundary cases (McNemar $p{=}.014$), provides calibrated probabilities (ECE\\,=\\,0.0027; \\S\\ref{sec:calibration}), and retains 97.65\\% under marker removal vs.\\ B5's 28.49\\% (\\S\\ref{sec:nomarker}).\nThe residual 2/1190 errors (both \\texttt{GeneratorExit}; Appendix~\\ref{sec:irreducible}) are shared across seeds; CodeLlama-13B seed 123 achieves 100\\%.\n\n\\paragraph{Design implications.}\nThe two-tier hierarchy (Table~\\ref{tab:cross_model}) suggests a practical fallback: extract exception events first (explicit pathway); resort to tail-truncated execution state when no exception is raised (implicit pathway).\nTier~2 benefit is model-dependent (Qwen +10.9/+7.2pp at 7B/1.5B; CodeLlama +0.9pp), so trace design should match deployment model capacity.\nWhether this transfers to other trace-based tasks \\citep{gu2024cruxeval,ni2024next} remains open.\n\n\\paragraph{Caveats.}\nThe decomposition (\\S\\ref{sec:density}) is a preliminary upper-bound estimate on the Qwen family only; CodeLlama and DeepSeek exhibit label-only collapse (28.5\\%), preventing cross-family replication.\nThe diagnostic token ratio captures only the explicit pathway; $N{=}4$ models is insufficient for a continuous density--accuracy relationship.\nHumanEval/MBPP covers only simple assert-based tests; real-world failure modes (integration tests, parametric tests, flaky tests) remain untested.\n",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01K7FSLUSHPB4DxKSLjeS48P`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	
3	\paragraph{Execution Traces for Code LLMs.}
4	TracePile \citep{chen2025tracepile} scaled trace-augmented training to 2.6M samples; \citet{wang2025dcsh} found trace utility task- and format-dependent, which we extend by identifying exception events as the dominant signal.
5	NExT \citep{ni2024next} and ET-CoT \citep{eguchi2025etcot} used trace chain-of-thought for repair and execution reasoning; \citet{ferrando2025execute} found compact traces comparable to full traces, supporting our density finding.
6	\citet{liu2023codeexecution} pre-trained on execution traces; CRUXEval \citep{gu2024cruxeval}, extended to 19 languages \citep{xu2025cruxevalx}, established execution reasoning benchmarks.
7	None decompose which trace components contribute to \emph{classification} accuracy.
8	
9	\paragraph{Code Verdict and Patch Classification.}
10	TRACED \citep{ding2024traced} showed execution-aware pretraining improves vulnerability detection; CC2Vec \citep{hoang2020cc2vec}, LLM4PatchCorrect \citep{zhou2024llm4patch}, and APPT \citep{appt2024} developed representation learning of code changes.
11	Verdict prediction is an instance of the test oracle problem \citep{barr2015oracle}; none isolate individual trace components.
12	
13	\paragraph{Fault Localization and Trace Selection.}
14	SBFL \citep{abreu2007accuracy,jones2002visualization,wong2014dstar} and LLM-based FL \citep{kang2024autofl,qin2024agentfl} share our insight that anomalous execution events carry the strongest diagnostic signal.
15	TraceCoder \citep{huang2026tracecoder} and InspectCoder \citep{wang2025inspectcoder} use selective trace extraction; RepairAgent \citep{bouzenia2025repairagent}, Agentless \citep{xia2024agentless}, SWE-agent \citep{yang2024sweagent}, and OpenHands \citep{wang2025openhands} incorporate execution feedback in repair workflows \citep{jimenez2024swebench}, yet which trace components carry verdict signals most efficiently remains unstudied.
16	Prompt compression \citep{jiang2023llmlingua,li2023selectivecontext} showed pruning low-information tokens preserves accuracy; our decomposition quantifies \emph{why}.
17	
18	\paragraph{Calibration, Data Selection, and Defect Prediction.}
19	Neural network calibration \citep{guo2017calibration}, extended to LLMs \citep{kadavath2022language,geng2024calibration,spiess2025calibration}, studies confidence-correctness alignment; our ECE ${<}$ 0.005 suggests compact inputs improve calibration.
20	Curriculum learning \citep{bengio2009curriculum}, dataset cartography \citep{swayamdipta2020cartography}, data selection \citep{sachdeva2024dataefficient,xie2023doremi}, and data pruning \citep{paul2021deep,sorscher2022beyond} show that removing low-value examples improves convergence, consistent with our finding that compact exceptions converge rapidly while verbose traces oscillate.
21	Just-in-time defect prediction \citep{kamei2013large,hoang2019deepjit} shares our fault detection motivation.
22	
```

</details>

        "content": "\\section{Related Work}\n\n\\paragraph{Execution Traces for Code LLMs.}\nTracePile \\citep{chen2025tracepile} scaled trace-augmented training to 2.6M samples; \\citet{wang2025dcsh} found trace utility task- and format-dependent, which we extend by identifying exception events as the dominant signal.\nNExT \\citep{ni2024next} and ET-CoT \\citep{eguchi2025etcot} used trace chain-of-thought for repair and execution reasoning; \\citet{ferrando2025execute} found compact traces comparable to full traces, supporting our density finding.\n\\citet{liu2023codeexecution} pre-trained on execution traces; CRUXEval \\citep{gu2024cruxeval,xu2025cruxevalx} established execution reasoning benchmarks.\nNone decompose which trace components contribute to \\emph{classification} accuracy.\n\n\\paragraph{Code Verdict and Patch Classification.}\nTRACED \\citep{ding2024traced} showed execution-aware pretraining improves vulnerability detection; CC2Vec \\citep{hoang2020cc2vec}, LLM4PatchCorrect \\citep{zhou2024llm4patch}, and APPT \\citep{appt2024} developed representation learning of code changes.\nVerdict prediction is an instance of the test oracle problem \\citep{barr2015oracle}; none isolate individual trace components.\n\n\\paragraph{Fault Localization and Trace Selection.}\nSBFL \\citep{abreu2007accuracy,jones2002visualization,wong2014dstar} and LLM-based FL \\citep{kang2024autofl,qin2024agentfl} share our insight that anomalous execution events carry the strongest diagnostic signal.\nTraceCoder \\citep{huang2026tracecoder} and InspectCoder \\citep{wang2025inspectcoder} use selective trace extraction; RepairAgent \\citep{bouzenia2025repairagent}, Agentless \\citep{xia2024agentless}, SWE-agent \\citep{yang2024sweagent}, and OpenHands \\citep{wang2025openhands} incorporate execution feedback in repair workflows \\citep{jimenez2024swebench}, yet which trace components carry verdict signals most efficiently remains unstudied.\nPrompt compression \\citep{jiang2023llmlingua,li2023selectivecontext} showed pruning low-information tokens preserves accuracy; our decomposition quantifies \\emph{why}.\n\n\\paragraph{Calibration, Data Selection, and Defect Prediction.}\nNeural network calibration \\citep{guo2017calibration}, extended to LLMs \\citep{kadavath2022language,geng2024calibration,spiess2025calibration}, studies confidence-correctness alignment; our ECE ${<}$ 0.005 suggests compact inputs improve calibration.\nCurriculum learning \\citep{bengio2009curriculum}, dataset cartography \\citep{swayamdipta2020cartography}, data selection \\citep{sachdeva2024dataefficient,xie2023doremi}, and data pruning \\citep{paul2021deep,sorscher2022beyond} show that removing low-value examples improves convergence, consistent with our finding that compact exceptions converge rapidly while verbose traces oscillate.\nJust-in-time defect prediction \\citep{kamei2013large} shares our fault detection motivation.\n",
        "numLines": 22,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 22
      }
    },

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/discussion.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Discussion}\n\\label{sec:discussion}\n\n\\paragraph{A two-pathway model of verdict signals.}\nThe results suggest execution traces contain two independent signal pathways for verdict prediction, whose interaction explains why complete traces degrade SFT learning.\n\n\\paragraph{Explicit pathway: diagnostic tokens.}\nException and assertion events directly encode fault occurrence.\nThe diagnostic token ratio $d$ (\\S2.1) correlates with accuracy at $r{=}0.949$ ($p{=}0.014$) across five conditions, and all four models achieve ${\\geq}$99.33\\% under \\texttt{exception\\_only} with zero variance on Qwen ($\\sigma{=}$0.00, 3 seeds).\nWe acknowledge that exception events structurally encode verdict information; the contribution is systematic quantification across four models and three families.\nAlthough 55.5\\% of failures are silent logic errors, \\texttt{exception\\_only} achieves 100\\% fail recall because test assertions generate \\texttt{AssertionError} events, confirming the pathway captures both crashes and assertion failures.\n\\texttt{ae\\_only}, a strict superset, slightly underperforms \\texttt{exception\\_only} (Table~\\ref{tab:ablation}), confirming that additional content introduces noise.\n\n\\paragraph{Implicit pathway: terminal execution state.}\nTail-truncated traces achieve 93--97\\% accuracy (Table~\\ref{tab:tail_cross_model}) despite containing near-zero diagnostic tokens ($d {\\approx} 0$).\nThe mechanism: whole-input tail truncation at ${\\geq}$512 tokens captures \\texttt{[Line~X]} variable values at final execution steps, which indirectly encode program outcome.\nBelow 384 tokens, the globals module dump consumes the entire budget, yielding ${\\sim}$73\\% ($\\approx$ \\texttt{no\\_trace}).\nThe +13.8pp phase transition at tail-384$\\to$tail-512 marks where the model crosses this noise floor and accesses terminal execution state.\nThis pathway is genuinely non-obvious: it operates on structured program state with zero explicit fault markers.\n\n\\paragraph{Noise dilution in complete traces.}\nBoth pathways' signals exist in full traces but are buried under thousands of intermediate tokens.\nFigure~\\ref{fig:training_dynamics} confirms: \\texttt{exception\\_only} converges within 50 steps, while \\texttt{full\\_trace} with label-only loss oscillates (82--89\\%) without convergence (Appendix~\\ref{sec:training_dynamics}); seed variance negatively correlates with diagnostic density.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{training_dynamics}\n\\caption{Training dynamics: \\texttt{exception\\_only} converges to 99.83\\% by step 150; \\texttt{full\\_trace} with label-only loss oscillates (82--89\\%) without convergence.}\n\\label{fig:training_dynamics}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{Why SFT over regex?}\nSFT \\texttt{exception\\_only} (99.83\\%) exceeds the best regex (B\\_marker, 99.33\\%) by 0.50pp on 6 caught-exception boundary cases (McNemar $p{=}.014$), provides calibrated probabilities (ECE\\,=\\,0.0027; \\S\\ref{sec:calibration}), and retains 97.65\\% under marker removal vs.\\ B5's 28.49\\% (\\S\\ref{sec:nomarker}).\nThe residual 2/1190 errors (both \\texttt{GeneratorExit}; Appendix~\\ref{sec:irreducible}) are shared across seeds; CodeLlama-13B seed 123 achieves 100\\%.\n\n\\paragraph{Design implications.}\nThe two-tier hierarchy (Table~\\ref{tab:cross_model}) suggests a practical fallback: extract exception events first (explicit pathway); resort to tail-truncated execution state when no exception is raised (implicit pathway).\nTier~2 benefit is model-dependent (Qwen +10.9/+7.2pp at 7B/1.5B; CodeLlama +0.9pp), so trace design should match deployment model capacity.\nWhether this transfers to other trace-based tasks \\citep{gu2024cruxeval,ni2024next} remains open.\n\n\\paragraph{Caveats.}\nThe decomposition (\\S\\ref{sec:density}) is a preliminary upper-bound estimate on the Qwen family only (CodeLlama and DeepSeek exhibit label-only collapse); $N{=}4$ models is insufficient for a continuous density--accuracy relationship.\nHumanEval/MBPP covers only simple assert-based tests; real-world failure modes remain untested.\n",
        "numLines": 44,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 44

      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/conclusion.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Conclusion}\n\nAcross four models (1.5B--13B, three families), complete traces (77--82\\%) underperform compact exception events (${\\geq}$99.33\\%) by 18+pp; a decomposition attributes ${\\geq}$74\\% of the gap to information overload at inference.\nA two-pathway model---\\emph{explicit} (exception/assertion events; ${\\geq}$99.33\\%) and \\emph{implicit} (terminal execution state; 93--97\\%)---with a cross-family two-tier hierarchy (12+pp gap, non-overlapping CIs) suggests a practical fallback: exception events first, tail-truncated state otherwise.\nGeneralization to human-written code, other languages, non-assert tests, and larger models remains open.\n",
        "numLines": 6,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 6
      }
    },

[Director 指令] **[Comprehensive Rewrite] Round 15 Response — EMNLP 2026, DDL 5/25**

Round 15 审稿 Overall 3.0 (Findings)。4 Must-Fix + 8 Should-Fix。核心问题：contribution framing 15 轮未解决。需要一次性结构性重写。

## 核心 Reframe 方向（已审批）

**从 "exceptions help" 转为 "complete traces hurt"**。具体：

### Contribution 重构（4 → 4，内容大改）

**旧 C1**: "Exception events as minimal sufficient signal (≥99.33%)"
**新 C1**: "Complete traces (77-82%) are strictly dominated by compact exception events (≥99.33%) — a surprising finding given the additional information complete traces contain"

**旧 C2**: "Two-Pathway Model — diagnostic token ratio"
**新 C2**: "SFT exception_only achieves ≥97.65% even without format markers (vs regex baseline 28.49%), and transfers cross-dataset at 99.41% — demonstrating genuine pattern recognition beyond format matching"

**旧 C3**: "Preliminary decomposition of full_trace degradation"
**新 C3**: "A two-tier diagnostic signal hierarchy holds across model families, yielding a practical trace selection strategy with a sharp performance transition at ~512 tail tokens"

**旧 C4**: "Two-pathway model"
**新 C4**: "Preliminary decomposition suggests ≥74% of the degradation originates at inference time (confounded upper-bound estimate with explicit caveats)"

### 重写优先级和要求

#### Phase 1: 结构重写（立即开始，不等实验数据）

**1. Abstract 重写**
- 开头：verdict prediction task → finding = complete traces degrade SFT
- 核心数字：full_trace 77-82% vs exception_only ≥99.33% vs no_trace ~75% → complete traces WORSE than no traces for some models
- B5 regex 99.33% → 承认 format markers enable simple regex, BUT nomarker ablation (97.65% vs 28.49%) + cross-dataset (99.41%) prove SFT learns genuine patterns
- Decomposition：改为 "preliminary confounded estimate" 语言
- 结论：practical design principle — extract diagnostic signals, avoid complete traces

**2. Introduction 重写**
- Para 1-2: motivation — 为什么研究 trace representation matters
- Key shift: 不再以 "exception_only beats everything" 开头，而是 "complete traces, despite containing all information, systematically degrade SFT performance"
- B5 regex 作为 PRIMARY baseline 引入（Section 3 之前就提及），framing 为 "even a simple regex achieves 99.33% — the interesting question is WHY complete traces hurt, not why exceptions help"
- Contribution list: 用上面的新 C1-C4

**3. §3.1 或新增 subsection: B5 as Primary Baseline**
- Table 1 新增 B5 regex 行（目前 buried in Appendix P）
- 正文明确：B5 regex (99.33%) 利用 [No exception data in trace] marker
- **然后立即接 nomarker ablation**：去掉 marker 后 B5 → 28.49%，SFT → 97.65%（-2.01pp）。这证明 SFT 学到了 beyond format matching 的 genuine diagnostic pattern recognition。
- 将 nomarker ablation 从 §3.3 附带提及提升为 formal ablation with its own paragraph

**4. §3.4 Decomposition 降级**
- 标题改为 "Preliminary Decomposition (Confounded Upper-Bound Estimate)"
- 正文列出全部 confounders: (a) gradient selection — loss computed on ~1/500 tokens, (b) implicit regularization — removing NTP on trace tokens, (c) effective LR — ~2000x increase per label token
- "74% information overload" → "≤74% attributable to inference-time content effects (confounded upper bound; the label-only manipulation simultaneously changes gradient selection, implicit regularization, and effective learning rate)"
- 删除任何 clean causal 暗示
- "Two-pathway model" → "two-pathway descriptive framework" / "working hypothesis"。保留名称但每次使用时加 "descriptive" 或 "preliminary"

**5. Discussion 修改**
- 新增 ML theory 段（2-3 句）：连接 Information Bottleneck (Tishby et al., 2000), feature selection (Guyon & Elisseeff, 2003), LIMA (Zhou et al., 2023)。定位为 "our findings provide empirical evidence that the classical 'more features can hurt' principle manifests in SFT on execution traces"
- 新增 no_trace baseline 讨论段：93%+ from source code alone → traces' total incremental value is ~7pp → but complete traces are WORSE → the degradation is the interesting finding
- "information overload" → 首次出现加引号保留 intuition；后续全部替换为 "diagnostic signal dilution" 或 "irrelevant feature noise"
- "gradient dilution eliminated" → "gradient dilution reduced" （检查 discussion.tex 是否有残留）

**6. Conclusion 扩充**
- 现在 4 行太少。扩充 2-3 句：broader implications for trace-based tool design + connection to classical ML principle + next step (controlled gradient masking ablation)
- 控制在 page 8 以内

**7. Limitations 扩写**
- 承认 3 seeds 是 minimum
- 承认依赖 assert-based test structure（HumanEval+MBPP = simple algorithmic tasks）
- 承认 1.5B-13B scale，30B+ may overcome noise dilution
- Full_trace 未在 DebugBench 上评估
- Decomposition confounders 未完全分离（future work: random gradient masking）

#### Phase 2: 数据集成（等实验结果，后续 Patch）

以下数据尚在计算中，Phase 1 完成后我会派 Patch：
- **Cross-dataset 完整结果**（HE→MBPP done: exc=99.41%, no_trace=70.56%; MBPP→HE ~30min）→ 新 Table 或 subsection
- **Holm-Bonferroni adjusted p-values** → Table 1 脚注或新列
- **Problem-level clustered bootstrap CI** → 替换当前 CI（clustered N=1190 as primary）
- **Oscillation metrics**（rolling variance, CV）→ Figure 3 caption 或 appendix
- **k-means tier validation** → 如果 k=2 显著则加入；不显著则不写
- **SF-4 完整 training dynamics figure** → 替换当前 Figure 3

#### 必须同步修复的遗留问题

**C1 (traceback 语言残留)**: Reviewer 报告 experiments.tex 2 处 + appendix.tex 3 处仍写 "exception traceback length"。你之前说已全部修复（grep 0 残留）。**请再次 grep 确认**，如有残留立即修正为 "globals module dump threshold" / "[Line X] structural markers"。

**C3 (DeepSeek tail-384)**: 检查 appendix table 是否仍写 82.9%（应为 80.7% point estimate）。experiments.tex "+11.0--22.0pp" 应为 "+13.2--22.0pp"。appendix caption "+13.7--22.0pp" 统一为 "+13.2--22.0pp"。

#### 其他 Should-Fix / Minor

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01LhxNQUMUJbZvHxANS8aJ5J`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: paper-writing-norms

4	allowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]
5	scope: global
6	version: 2
7	priority: 9

9	  - writing
10	  - paper
11	  - style
12	  - norms
13	roles:
14	  - agent
15	  - worker
16	  - paper_agent
17	---
18	
19	# 论文写作风格规范
20	
21	> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## 一、总体写作哲学
26	
27	### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌
28	
29	- 论文必须以"故事线"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证
30	- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进
31	- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌
32	- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing
33	
34	### 1.2 主线统一性
35	
36	- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**
37	- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C
38	- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机
39	- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线
40	
41	### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义
42	
43	- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释
44	- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）
45	- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用
46	- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug
47	
48	### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文
49	
50	- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可
51	- 重点学：
52	  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事
53	  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献
54	  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）
55	  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度
56	- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大
57	- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文
58	
59	---
60	
61	## 二、Abstract 写作规范
62	
63	### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）
64	
65	Abstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：
66	1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点
67	2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）
68	3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）
69	4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献
70	
71	### 2.2 禁忌
72	
73	- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim
74	- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 "revolutionary"、"groundbreaking"
75	- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元
76	- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了
77	- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开
78	
79	### 2.3 审稿人视角检验
80	
81	写完后用这个标准自检：审稿人能否在 30 秒内判断——
82	- 问题是否重要？
83	- 方法是否清楚？
84	- 贡献是否可信？
85	- 结果是否有说服力？
86	
87	---
88	
89	## 三、Introduction 写作规范
90	
91	### 3.1 完整叙事线（5 段结构）
92	
93	Introduction 需要从一个清晰、重要的问题切入，逐步把读者带到核心贡献：
94	
95	1. **研究背景**（1-2 段）：大模型在某类能力/应用中已取得进展，但仍存在一个关键瓶颈
96	2. **问题重要性**（1 段）：结合真实应用、已有方法局限或当前趋势，说明这不是小问题
97	3. **现有方法不足**（1 段）：系统梳理为什么不够（依赖更大模型/更多数据/更复杂推理/更高成本，或只在特定 benchmark 有效）
98	4. **本文核心想法**（1 段）：我们观察到什么、提出了什么方法、为什么能解决上述问题
99	5. **贡献总结**（列表）：清楚列出方法贡献、实验贡献和分析贡献
100	
101	### 3.2 整体叙事结构
102	
103	```
104	现有进展 → 关键缺口 → 本文洞察 → 方法概述 → 实验证据 → 贡献总结
105	```
106	
107	### 3.3 禁忌
108	
109	- **禁止泛泛介绍**：不要开头就写 "LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities..."，要从具体问题切入
110	- **禁止缺少过渡**：每段之间必须有逻辑连接
111	- **禁止实验报告风格**：Introduction 不是 "我们做了 X 实验得到了 Y 结果" 的罗列
112	- 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑
113	- Contribution 列表中不能出现未在前文铺垫过的概念
114	
115	---
116	
117	## 四、Related Work 写作规范
118	
119	### 4.1 目标
120	
121	让审稿人相信：作者理解这个领域，知道自己的方法站在什么位置，清楚相对已有工作的真实增量在哪里。
122	
123	### 4.2 结构要求
124	
125	- 把已有工作分成几个清晰的主题（如模型训练、推理增强、工具调用、数据构建、评测等）
126	- 每类简要说明：已有方法做了什么、解决了哪些问题、和本文的关系
127	- 帮助读者理解：哪些是本文直接继承的，哪些方法不同但解决相似问题，哪些留下了本文要解决的缺口
128	
129	### 4.3 禁忌
130	
131	- **禁止论文堆砌**：不要写成 "A proposed X. B proposed Y. C proposed Z." 的列表
132	- **目标不是展示读了多少论文**，而是做归纳和比较
133	- Contribution claim（"To our knowledge, no prior work..."）应放在 Introduction，不在 Related Work
134	
135	### 4.4 引用充足度
136	
137	- 引用必须覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**
138	- 任何一类缺失，审稿人会立刻看出来调研不充分
139	- 引用密度参照目标会议近年录用论文的量级——明显偏少意味着 Related Work 调研不足
140	
141	---
142	
143	## 五、去 AI 写作风格（硬规则）
144	
145	以下模式是 AI 生成文本的典型标志，审稿人一眼能看出。**必须全部消除。**
146	
147	### 5.1 严禁破折号（最显著的 AI 痕迹）
148	
149	**这是 AI 写作最容易被识别的标志，审稿人最敏感。单列为最高优先级硬规则。**
150	
151	- 解释性 em-dash（—，U+2014）和 en-dash（–，U+2013）必须**全部删除**
152	- 替代方式：用逗号、括号或拆分为两个句子
153	- 强制检查命令：
154	  ```bash
155	  grep -n "—" paper/*.tex     # em-dash，每处人工判断是否必要
156	  grep -n " – " paper/*.tex   # 解释性 en-dash，几乎都应删除
157	  ```
158	- 例外（允许保留）：表格分隔符、数字范围（`2020–2024`、`pp. 12–18`）、专有名词（`Newton–Raphson`）
159	
160	### 5.2 段落与句式
161	
162	- **禁止过度分段**：不需要每 2-3 句就换段，自然段 5-8 句是正常的
163	- **减少关系从句**（which, that, who）：能用简洁直接的句子就不用从句
164	- **禁止 -ing 短语结尾**：如 "..., highlighting the applicability of..." / "..., demonstrating the effectiveness of..."。这是 AI 写作最典型的标志之一
165	- **禁止连续使用 "Furthermore" / "Moreover" / "Additionally"**：一段内最多用一个过渡词
166	- **避免 "It is worth noting that..." / "Notably, ..."**：直接写要说的内容
167	
168	### 5.3 用词黑名单
169	
170	- 避免 "delve into" / "leverage" / "utilize" / "facilitate" — 用 "explore" / "use" / "enable"
171	- 避免 "a myriad of" / "a plethora of" — 用 "many" / "various"
172	- 避免 "in the realm of" / "in the context of" — 直接说
173	- 避免 "paradigm shift" / "novel" 过度使用
174	
175	### 5.4 校对清单（4 步）
176	
177	1. 全文 proofread 语法和拼写
178	2. 检查所有代词/指示词（this, that, it）是否指代明确
179	3. **`grep "—"` + `grep " – "` 全文清零**（删除所有解释性 dash，§5.1）
180	4. `grep "ing,"` 模式——大部分 -ing 分词短语需要改写
181	
182	---
183	
184	## 六、图表与视觉规范
185	
186	### 6.1 知识密度原则
187	
188	- **图表的信息必须紧凑**——不能为了美观而留大量白空间
189	- 一张图如果只传达一个简单信息，要么缩小要么合并到其他图中
190	- 能用 inline text 说清的不要占一整个 Figure
191	
192	### 6.2 表格规范
193	
194	- 表格和正文文字不能视觉重叠（检查 column 宽度和 alignment）
195	- 小数精度全表统一
196	- 最优加粗、次优下划线
197	- Caption 必须自包含
198	
199	### 6.3 图片规范
200	
201	- 数值标签不能被线、bar、点遮住——用白色背景或偏移标签
202	- 图中字体 >= 7pt（打印后仍可读）
203	- 栏宽约束需匹配目标 venue 模板要求
204	- 概念图/流程图要有信息量，不要画一个 trivial 的 pipeline 然后占半页
205	
206	### 6.4 空间效率
207	
208	- 顶会正文页数严格受限（具体页数查目标会议官网），每 cm² 都是宝贵资源
209	- 优先使用 `\subfigure` 合并相关图
210	- Table 能 inline 就不要单独浮动
211	- 图表占比与其对叙事的贡献成正比
212	
213	### 6.5 页面节奏
214	
215	好论文的页面有节奏感——text、equation、figure 交替出现，读者视觉不疲劳：
216	
217	- **text-equation-figure 三重奏**：重要概念用"文字解释 → 公式定义 → 图表直觉"三步展开
218	- 连续 2 页纯文本（无 figure/equation/table）→ 穿插一个 display item 打破节奏
219	- 连续 2 个 figure 页（整页被 float 占满）→ 至少隔 1 段文字再放下一个 float
220	- Section heading 不孤悬页底（heading 后至少跟 2 行正文，否则强制分页）
221	- 最后一页内容 ≥ 2/3 页面高度（太空显得凑数，太满放不下 references）
222	
223	### 6.6 表格 LaTeX 规范
224	
225	**Booktabs 标准（硬规则）**：
226	- 用 `\toprule` / `\midrule` / `\bottomrule`，禁止 `\hline`，禁止竖线 `|`
227	- 用 `\centering` 不用 `\begin{center}`（后者多加垂直空白）
228	- 最优结果加粗（`\textbf{}`），次优下划线（`\underline{}`）+ 方向箭头 `↑`/`↓` 标明指标方向
229	- 小数精度全表统一（如全部保留 1 位或 2 位）
230	- Caption 在表格上方（`\caption` 在 `\begin{tabular}` 之前）
231	
232	**宽度决策树**：
233	- ≤5 列 → 单栏宽度（`\columnwidth`）
234	- 6–8 列 → 全文宽度（`\textwidth`，双栏模板用 `table*`）
235	- >8 列 → 拆成两张表或移入 appendix
236	
237	---
238	
239	## 七、投稿前通用准备
240	
241	### 7.1 模板必须官方（硬规则）
242	
243	- LaTeX 模板**必须从目标会议官网下载**（CallForPapers / Author Instructions 页面）
244	- **不要用**：
245	  - 网上他人转载的复制版本（可能被改过 margin / 行号设置）
246	  - 上一年的模板（参数可能变：字体、margin、行号、anonymous 选项等）
247	  - 组里学长留的旧版本（来源不明、无法追溯）
248	- 风险：
249	  - 样式不符合 → desk reject
250	  - margin 不对 → 被审稿人质疑诚信
251	  - reference 格式错 → 被认为不规范
252	- 流程：先查会议官网 templates → 找不到再 `WebSearch "{venue} {year} latex template official"` → 仍找不到汇报 Director（不要用就近年版本顶替）
253	- `\documentclass[]{}` 选项必须与目标 venue + 阶段（review / camera-ready）一致
254	
255	### 7.2 投稿要求逐项核对
256	
257	- 目标会议如要求 Checklist / Ethical Statement / Broader Impact Statement / Reproducibility Statement，必须**认真填每一项**，不要全写 N/A
258	- 页数限制：查目标会议官网
259	- 匿名化要求：review 版 vs camera-ready 版本设置不同
260	- 行号开启（review 版常需）
261	- 字体大小、margin 不得修改 sty 文件
262	
263	### 7.3 最终修改 10 项检查流程
264	
265	提交前必须逐一检查：
266	1. 所有最新实验结果已全部录入
267	2. 根据实验结果将叙事调至最优状态
268	3. 附录所有内容在正文中被引用且融入叙事
269	4. 无数据堆砌（每个数字服务于叙事推进）
270	5. Abstract 和 Introduction 具有吸引力（不是实验报告风格）
271	6. 全文主线统一（idea 变化后各章节逻辑一致）
272	7. 所有专有名词有定义
273	8. 无 AI 风格标志（dash、-ing phrases、过度分段）
274	9. 图表知识密度高、无视觉遮挡
275	10. Reference 覆盖直接相关方法、baseline、benchmark、理论基础
276	
````

</details>

      "content": "---\nname: paper-writing-norms\ndescription: 顶会级别论文写作风格规范——章节写法（Abstract/Intro/Related Work）、去 AI 风格、视觉密度、叙事哲学。Paper Agent 和 Worker 写论文章节时必须加载。\nallowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]\nscope: global\nversion: 2\npriority: 9\ntags:\n  - writing\n  - paper\n  - style\n  - norms\nroles:\n  - agent\n  - worker\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# 论文写作风格规范\n\n> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。\n\n---\n\n## 一、总体写作哲学\n\n### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌\n\n- 论文必须以\"故事线\"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证\n- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进\n- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌\n- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing\n\n### 1.2 主线统一性\n\n- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**\n- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C\n- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机\n- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线\n\n### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义\n\n- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释\n- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）\n- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用\n- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug\n\n### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文\n\n- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可\n- 重点学：\n  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事\n  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献\n  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）\n  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度\n- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大\n- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文\n\n---\n\n## 二、Abstract 写作规范\n\n### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）\n\nAbstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：\n1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点\n2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）\n3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）\n4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献\n\n### 2.2 禁忌\n\n- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim\n- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 \"revolutionary\"、\"groundbreaking\"\n- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元\n- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了\n- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开\n\n### 2.3 审稿人视角检验\n\n写完后用这个标准自检：审稿人能否在 30 秒内判断——\n- 问题是否重要？\n- 方法是否清楚？\n- 贡献是否可信？\n- 结果是否有说服力？\n\n---\n\n## 三、Introduction 写作规范\n\n### 3.1 完整叙事线（5 段结构）\n\nIntroduction 需要从一个清晰、重要的问题切入，逐步把读者带到核心贡献：\n\n1. **研究背景**（1-2 段）：大模型在某类能力/应用中已取得进展，但仍存在一个关键瓶颈\n2. **问题重要性**（1 段）：结合真实应用、已有方法局限或当前趋势，说明这不是小问题\n3. **现有方法不足**（1 段）：系统梳理为什么不够（依赖更大模型/更多数据/更复杂推理/更高成本，或只在特定 benchmark 有效）\n4. **本文核心想法**（1 段）：我们观察到什么、提出了什么方法、为什么能解决上述问题\n5. **贡献总结**（列表）：清楚列出方法贡献、实验贡献和分析贡献\n\n### 3.2 整体叙事结构\n\n```\n现有进展 → 关键缺口 → 本文洞察 → 方法概述 → 实验证据 → 贡献总结\n```\n\n### 3.3 禁忌\n\n- **禁止泛泛介绍**：不要开头就写 \"LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities...\"，要从具体问题切入\n- **禁止缺少过渡**：每段之间必须有逻辑连接\n- **禁止实验报告风格**：Introduction 不是 \"我们做了 X 实验得到了 Y 结果\" 的罗列\n- 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑\n- Contribution 列表中不能出现未在前文铺垫过的概念\n\n---\n\n## 四、Related Work 写作规范\n\n### 4.1 目标\n\n让审稿人相信：作者理解这个领域，知道自己的方法站在什么位置，清楚相对已有工作的真实增量在哪里。\n\n### 4.2 结构要求\n\n- 把已有工作分成几个清晰的主题（如模型训练、推理增强、工具调用、数据构建、评测等）\n- 每类简要说明：已有方法做了什么、解决了哪些问题、和本文的关系\n- 帮助读者理解：哪些是本文直接继承的，哪些方法不同但解决相似问题，哪些留下了本文要解决的缺口\n\n### 4.3 禁忌\n\n- **禁止论文堆砌**：不要写成 \"A proposed X. B proposed Y. C proposed Z.\" 的列表\n- **目标不是展示读了多少论文**，而是做归纳和比较\n- Contribution claim（\"To our knowledge, no prior work...\"）应放在 Introduction，不在 Related Work\n\n### 4.4 引用充足度\n\n- 引用必须覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**\n- 任何一类缺失，审稿人会立刻看出来调研不充分\n- 引用密度参照目标会议近年录用论文的量级——明显偏少意味着 Related Work 调研不足\n\n---\n\n## 五、去 AI 写作风格（硬规则）\n\n以下模式是 AI 生成文本的典型标志，审稿人一眼能看出。**必须全部消除。**\n\n### 5.1 严禁破折号（最显著的 AI 痕迹）\n\n**这是 AI 写作最容易被识别的标志，审稿人最敏感。单列为最高优先级硬规则。**\n\n- 解释性 em-dash（—，U+2014）和 en-dash（–，U+2013）必须**全部删除**\n- 替代方式：用逗号、括号或拆分为两个句子\n- 强制检查命令：\n  ```bash\n  grep -n \"—\" paper/*.tex     # em-dash，每处人工判断是否必要\n  grep -n \" – \" paper/*.tex   # 解释性 en-dash，几乎都应删除\n  ```\n- 例外（允许保留）：表格分隔符、数字范围（`2020–2024`、`pp. 12–18`）、专有名词（`Newton–Raphson`）\n\n### 5.2 段落与句式\n\n- **禁止过度分段**：不需要每 2-3 句就换段，自然段 5-8 句是正常的\n- **减少关系从句**（which, that, who）：能用简洁直接的句子就不用从句\n- **禁止 -ing 短语结尾**：如 \"..., highlighting the applicability of...\" / \"..., demonstrating the effectiveness of...\"。这是 AI 写作最典型的标志之一\n- **禁止连续使用 \"Furthermore\" / \"Moreover\" / \"Additionally\"**：一段内最多用一个过渡词\n- **避免 \"It is worth noting that...\" / \"Notably, ...\"**：直接写要说的内容\n\n### 5.3 用词黑名单\n\n- 避免 \"delve into\" / \"leverage\" / \"utilize\" / \"facilitate\" — 用 \"explore\" / \"use\" / \"enable\"\n- 避免 \"a myriad of\" / \"a plethora of\" — 用 \"many\" / \"various\"\n- 避免 \"in the realm of\" / \"in the context of\" — 直接说\n- 避免 \"paradigm shift\" / \"novel\" 过度使用\n\n### 5.4 校对清单（4 步）\n\n1. 全文 proofread 语法和拼写\n2. 检查所有代词/指示词（this, that, it）是否指代明确\n3. **`grep \"—\"` + `grep \" – \"` 全文清零**（删除所有解释性 dash，§5.1）\n4. `grep \"ing,\"` 模式——大部分 -ing 分词短语需要改写\n\n---\n\n## 六、图表与视觉规范\n\n### 6.1 知识密度原则\n\n- **图表的信息必须紧凑**——不能为了美观而留大量白空间\n- 一张图如果只传达一个简单信息，要么缩小要么合并到其他图中\n- 能用 inline text 说清的不要占一整个 Figure\n\n### 6.2 表格规范\n\n- 表格和正文文字不能视觉重叠（检查 column 宽度和 alignment）\n- 小数精度全表统一\n- 最优加粗、次优下划线\n- Caption 必须自包含\n\n### 6.3 图片规范\n\n- 数值标签不能被线、bar、点遮住——用白色背景或偏移标签\n- 图中字体 >= 7pt（打印后仍可读）\n- 栏宽约束需匹配目标 venue 模板要求\n- 概念图/流程图要有信息量，不要画一个 trivial 的 pipeline 然后占半页\n\n### 6.4 空间效率\n\n- 顶会正文页数严格受限（具体页数查目标会议官网），每 cm² 都是宝贵资源\n- 优先使用 `\\subfigure` 合并相关图\n- Table 能 inline 就不要单独浮动\n- 图表占比与其对叙事的贡献成正比\n\n### 6.5 页面节奏\n\n好论文的页面有节奏感——text、equation、figure 交替出现，读者视觉不疲劳：\n\n- **text-equation-figure 三重奏**：重要概念用\"文字解释 → 公式定义 → 图表直觉\"三步展开\n- 连续 2 页纯文本（无 figure/equation/table）→ 穿插一个 display item 打破节奏\n- 连续 2 个 figure 页（整页被 float 占满）→ 至少隔 1 段文字再放下一个 float\n- Section heading 不孤悬页底（heading 后至少跟 2 行正文，否则强制分页）\n- 最后一页内容 ≥ 2/3 页面高度（太空显得凑数，太满放不下 references）\n\n### 6.6 表格 LaTeX 规范\n\n**Booktabs 标准（硬规则）**：\n- 用 `\\toprule` / `\\midrule` / `\\bottomrule`，禁止 `\\hline`，禁止竖线 `|`\n- 用 `\\centering` 不用 `\\begin{center}`（后者多加垂直空白）\n- 最优结果加粗（`\\textbf{}`），次优下划线（`\\underline{}`）+ 方向箭头 `↑`/`↓` 标明指标方向\n- 小数精度全表统一（如全部保留 1 位或 2 位）\n- Caption 在表格上方（`\\caption` 在 `\\begin{tabular}` 之前）\n\n**宽度决策树**：\n- ≤5 列 → 单栏宽度（`\\columnwidth`）\n- 6–8 列 → 全文宽度（`\\textwidth`，双栏模板用 `table*`）\n- >8 列 → 拆成两张表或移入 appendix\n\n---\n\n## 七、投稿前通用准备\n\n### 7.1 模板必须官方（硬规则）\n\n- LaTeX 模板**必须从目标会议官网下载**（CallForPapers / Author Instructions 页面）\n- **不要用**：\n  - 网上他人转载的复制版本（可能被改过 margin / 行号设置）\n  - 上一年的模板（参数可能变：字体、margin、行号、anonymous 选项等）\n  - 组里学长留的旧版本（来源不明、无法追溯）\n- 风险：\n  - 样式不符合 → desk reject\n  - margin 不对 → 被审稿人质疑诚信\n  - reference 格式错 → 被认为不规范\n- 流程：先查会议官网 templates → 找不到再 `WebSearch \"{venue} {year} latex template official\"` → 仍找不到汇报 Director（不要用就近年版本顶替）\n- `\\documentclass[]{}` 选项必须与目标 venue + 阶段（review / camera-ready）一致\n\n### 7.2 投稿要求逐项核对\n\n- 目标会议如要求 Checklist / Ethical Statement / Broader Impact Statement / Reproducibility Statement，必须**认真填每一项**，不要全写 N/A\n- 页数限制：查目标会议官网\n- 匿名化要求：review 版 vs camera-ready 版本设置不同\n- 行号开启（review 版常需）\n- 字体大小、margin 不得修改 sty 文件\n\n### 7.3 最终修改 10 项检查流程\n\n提交前必须逐一检查：\n1. 所有最新实验结果已全部录入\n2. 根据实验结果将叙事调至最优状态\n3. 附录所有内容在正文中被引用且融入叙事\n4. 无数据堆砌（每个数字服务于叙事推进）\n5. Abstract 和 Introduction 具有吸引力（不是实验报告风格）\n6. 全文主线统一（idea 变化后各章节逻辑一致）\n7. 所有专有名词有定义\n8. 无 AI 风格标志（dash、-ing phrases、过度分段）\n9. 图表知识密度高、无视觉遮挡\n10. Reference 覆盖直接相关方法、baseline、benchmark、理论基础\n",
      "numLines": 276,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 276
    }
  },

/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/experiments.tex:156:Cross-scale validation on Qwen-1.5B yields a consistent pattern (17\% gradient dilution, 83\% information overload, 3 seeds), confirming that information overload dominates across model scales.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/experiments.tex:158:Error analysis provides direct evidence: all 217 \texttt{full\_trace} errors occur in samples where tokenizer truncation removes diagnostic markers, and among the 61 samples where \texttt{full\_trace} underperforms \texttt{no\_trace}, all exhibit the same truncation pattern, confirming that information overload manifests through a mechanical truncation pathway rather than semantic confusion.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/main.tex:24:A preliminary decomposition on the Qwen family (two scales, three seeds each) suggests gradient dilution accounts for at most ${\sim}$26\% of the gap, with the remainder attributable to information overload at inference, though label-only loss simultaneously alters multiple training dynamics.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/conclusion.tex:3:Across four models (1.5B--13B, three families), complete traces (77--82\%) underperform compact exception events (${\geq}$99.33\%) by 18+pp; a decomposition attributes ${\geq}$74\% of the gap to information overload at inference.

/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/introduction.tex:19:A preliminary decomposition (Qwen family, 2 scales, 3 seeds each) provides an upper-bound estimate: gradient dilution accounts for at most ${\sim}$26\% of the gap, with the remainder reflecting information overload, though label-only loss simultaneously alters gradient selection, regularization, and training signal distribution (\S\ref{sec:density}).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/introduction.tex:27:\item \textbf{Preliminary decomposition of the full-trace degradation}: a label-only loss ablation on the Qwen family (7B and 1.5B, 3 seeds each) provides an upper-bound estimate: gradient dilution ${\leq}$26\%, information overload ${\geq}$74\%, though the ablation simultaneously changes multiple training dynamics (\S\ref{sec:density}). the remaining gap to VOTA reflects information overload at inference, but the decomposition is approximate (\\S\\ref{sec:density}).\n/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/introduction.tex:19:A preliminary decomposition (Qwen family, 2 scales, 3 seeds each) provides an upper-bound estimate: gradient dilution accounts for at most ${\\sim}$26\\% of the gap, with the remainder reflecting information overload, though label-only loss simultaneously alters gradient selection, regularization, and training signal distribution (\\S\\ref{sec:density}).\n/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/introduction.tex:27:\\item \\textbf{Preliminary decomposition of the full-trace degradation}: a label-only loss ablation on the Qwen family (7B and 1.5B, 3 seeds each) provides an upper-bound estimate: gradient dilution ${\\leq}$26\\%, information overload ${\\geq}$74\\%, though the ablation simultaneously changes multiple training dynamics (\\S\\ref{sec:density}). A budget sweep across four models reveals a sharp performance jump at ${\\sim}$500 tail tokens (\\S\\ref{sec:trace_length}).",

1	\documentclass[11pt,a4paper]{article}
2	\usepackage[review]{acl}
3	% \usepackage{acl}  % uncomment for camera-ready (remove [review])
4	\usepackage{times,latexsym}
5	\usepackage{amsmath,amssymb}
6	\usepackage{graphicx}
7	\graphicspath{{../../figures/paper/}}
8	\usepackage{booktabs}
9	\usepackage{multirow}
10	\usepackage{xcolor}
11	\usepackage{algorithm}
12	\usepackage{algorithmic}
13	
14	\title{Complete Execution Traces Degrade SFT-Based Verdict Prediction: \\A Diagnostic Signal Analysis}
15	\author{Anonymous ACL submission}
16	
17	\begin{document}
18	\maketitle
19	
20	\begin{abstract}
21	Complete execution traces contain all information needed for verdict prediction (classifying whether a test passes or fails), yet they actively impair SFT learning.
22	On LLM-generated Python code, across four models spanning three architecture families (1.5B--13B), complete traces achieve only 77--82\% accuracy despite containing strictly more diagnostic information than compact exception events (${\geq}$99.33\%), an 18+pp degradation from \emph{more} input.
23	Training dynamics reveal the mechanism: verbose traces cause models to overfit to spurious patterns. \texttt{full\_trace} with label-only loss (gradient dilution reduced) oscillates between 82--89\% across all training steps without stable convergence, while \texttt{exception\_only} converges within 50 steps.
24	A preliminary decomposition on the Qwen family (two scales, three seeds each) suggests gradient dilution accounts for at most ${\sim}$26\% of the gap, with the remainder attributable to information overload at inference, though label-only loss simultaneously alters multiple training dynamics.
25	A sharp performance jump at ${\sim}$500 tail tokens across all four models marks where tail truncation crosses the globals module dump threshold, exposing structured execution markers (\texttt{[Line~X]} variable values).
26	A two-tier signal hierarchy holds cross-family: Tier~1 (exceptions, assertions; ${\geq}$98.53\%) and Tier~2 (loop compression; 77--86\%) are separated by 12+pp with non-overlapping confidence intervals.
27	These results demonstrate that for SFT-based verdict prediction, more trace information impairs learning efficiency; the binding constraint is information density, not quantity.
28	\end{abstract}
29	
30	\input{introduction}
31	\input{method}
32	\input{experiments}
33	\input{discussion}
34	\input{related_work}
35	\input{conclusion}
36	
37	\section*{Limitations}
38	
39	Our study has several limitations.
40	First, all experiments use HumanEval and MBPP-sanitized (591 problems, simple assert-based tests on short Python functions); real-world failure modes including integration tests, parametric tests, and flaky tests remain untested, and generalization to human-written code, other languages, and multi-file projects is unverified.
41	Second, all four models (1.5B--13B) are code-specialized; whether these findings extend to general-purpose LLMs or larger scales is untested.
42	Third, our analysis is limited to verdict prediction; whether the two-pathway model transfers to other trace-based tasks (e.g., output prediction, program repair) is an open question.
43	
44	\section*{Ethics Statement}
45	
46	This work uses publicly available benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP-sanitized, DebugBench) and does not involve human subjects, crowdsourced annotations, or personally identifiable information.
47	All models evaluated are open-source and publicly accessible.
48	Training uses parameter-efficient LoRA fine-tuning on enterprise-grade GPUs, with each experiment using a single GPU.
49	Total compute is approximately 320 GPU-hours across all conditions, models, seeds, and iterative refinements (including approximately 170 hours for successful experiments; individual runs range from 0.5h for small models to 2.2h for CodeLlama-13B, with the total reflecting 4 models $\times$ multiple conditions $\times$ multiple seeds $\times$ iterative development).
50	We do not foresee direct negative societal impacts from this work; verdict prediction is a developer-facing tool for automated testing.
51	
52	\bibliography{references}
53	
54	\appendix
55	\input{appendix}
56	
57	\end{document}
58	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_019DqvanswWJAuGE8wkVBpA9`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	
3	Execution traces (line-by-line records of a program's runtime behavior) have emerged as a promising input modality for code language models \citep{chen2025tracepile,ding2024traced,wang2025dcsh,ni2024next}.
4	The intuition is straightforward: more execution information should help the model make better predictions.
5	Yet for verdict prediction (classifying whether a test passes or fails), this intuition is wrong.
6	Complete traces \emph{actively degrade} accuracy by 18+pp compared to compact exception events, despite containing strictly more diagnostic information.
7	\emph{Why does more information impair SFT learning?}
8	
9	Prior work found no single trace representation consistently outperforms others \citep{wang2025dcsh}, but focused on generation-oriented tasks.
10	Verdict prediction offers a cleaner testbed: as binary classification, it isolates the learning signal from generation noise, enabling controlled component ablation that directly measures how trace content affects SFT learning efficiency.
11	This proxy task aligns with prior *CL precedent: CRUXEval \citep{gu2024cruxeval} uses execution prediction to probe code reasoning, and NExT \citep{ni2024next} uses trace-augmented chain-of-thought for program repair.
12	
13	We present the first systematic component ablation of trace representations for verdict prediction on LLM-generated Python code.
14	Using 591 problems from HumanEval \citep{chen2021evaluating} and MBPP-sanitized \citep{austin2021program}, we decompose trace representations into individual components across four models spanning 1.5B to 13B parameters and three architecture families: Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B/1.5B-Instruct \citep{qwen2024coder}, CodeLlama-13B-Instruct \citep{roziere2024code}, and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct \citep{deepseek2024coder}.
15	
16	The central result: complete traces (77--82\% across four models) underperform compact exception events (${\geq}$99.33\%) by 18+pp despite containing all diagnostic information, directly demonstrating that more information impairs SFT learning efficiency.
17	Training dynamics confirm the mechanism: \texttt{exception\_only} converges within 50 steps, while \texttt{full\_trace} with label-only loss (gradient dilution reduced) oscillates (82--89\%) throughout training without reaching stable accuracy (Figure~\ref{fig:training_dynamics}).
18	
19	A preliminary decomposition (Qwen family, 2 scales, 3 seeds each) provides an upper-bound estimate: gradient dilution accounts for at most ${\sim}$26\% of the gap, with the remainder reflecting information overload, though label-only loss simultaneously alters gradient selection, regularization, and training signal distribution (\S\ref{sec:density}).
20	A tail-truncation budget sweep across all four models reveals a sharp performance jump at ${\sim}$500 tokens, crossing the globals module dump threshold (${\sim}$384 tokens) and exposing structured execution markers (\texttt{[Line~X]} variable values).
21	Even on silent logic errors (55.5\% of failures), exception events maintain 100\% recall because test assertions generate exception events.
22	
23	Our contributions:
24	\begin{enumerate}
25	\item \textbf{Complete traces actively impair SFT learning}: training dynamics directly show the mechanism: verbose traces cause overfitting to spurious patterns, with \texttt{full\_trace} oscillating (82--89\%) without stable convergence even when gradient dilution is eliminated. Seed variance negatively correlates with input density ($\sigma{=}$0.00 for \texttt{exception\_only} to 4.48 for \texttt{no\_trace}), and SFT closes a 6--49pp zero-shot gap across all models (\S\ref{sec:ablation}, Appendix~\ref{sec:training_dynamics}).
26	\item \textbf{Exception events are the minimal sufficient signal}: ${\geq}$99.33\% across all four models and all seeds tested (1.5B--13B, three families), with ${\sim}$91\% semantic rather than format-driven (\S\ref{sec:cross_model}, \S\ref{sec:nomarker}). Supplementary OOD analysis on DebugBench ($N{=}426$) preserves the ranking, though margins narrow due to ceiling effects (\S\ref{sec:debugbench}).
27	\item \textbf{Preliminary decomposition of the full-trace degradation}: a label-only loss ablation on the Qwen family (7B and 1.5B, 3 seeds each) provides an upper-bound estimate: gradient dilution ${\leq}$26\%, information overload ${\geq}$74\%, though the ablation simultaneously changes multiple training dynamics (\S\ref{sec:density}). A budget sweep across four models reveals a sharp performance jump at ${\sim}$500 tail tokens (\S\ref{sec:trace_length}).
28	\item \textbf{Two-tier diagnostic hierarchy validated across four models and three architecture families}: Tier~1 signals (exceptions, assertions) achieve ${\geq}$98.53\% across all four models with non-overlapping CIs against Tier~2; Tier~2 (loop compression, 77--86\%) is model-dependent: Qwen +10.9/+7.2pp (7B/1.5B), DeepSeek +7.0pp, CodeLlama +0.9pp (\S\ref{sec:ablation}).
29	\end{enumerate}
30	
```

1	\section{Experiments}
2	
3	We evaluate across two research questions:
4	(1)~Why do complete traces degrade SFT verdict accuracy despite containing all diagnostic information?
5	(2)~Which trace components carry the verdict signal, and how does their density affect learning?
6	All experiments use $N{=}1{,}190$ test samples; accuracy is reported with Wilson 95\% CIs.\footnote{Problem-level partition (472 train / 119 test, zero crossover; \S\ref{sec:dataset}). \texttt{exception\_only} duplicates (48.99\% test; Appendix~\ref{sec:dedup_stats}) arise from within-problem variants; deduplication yields 0.00pp delta. Problem-level clustered bootstrap CIs are ${\sim}2\times$ wider but all Tier~1 vs.\ Tier~2 gaps (${\geq}$12pp) remain significant ($p{<}.001$).}
7	Pairwise comparisons use paired bootstrap resampling ($B{=}10{,}000$, seed=42); all differences are significant at $p{<}.0001$ unless noted.
8

Full per-seed breakdowns in Appendix~\ref{sec:full_results}; per-class metrics in Appendix~\ref{sec:per_class}.}

36	\par\raggedright\footnotesize
37	DeepSeek \texttt{exception\_only} 3 seeds: 99.33/99.50/99.33 ($\sigma{=}$0.10pp).
38	VOTA on Qwen-7B: 99.41/98.66/99.08 ($\sigma{=}$0.38).
39	\texttt{ae\_only} 3 seeds: 99.92/99.08/99.92 (mean 99.64\%); Qwen-7B \texttt{loop\_only} 3 seeds: 85.63/85.88/86.64 (mean 86.05\%).

44	
45	\paragraph{Exception dominance across families.}
46	Under \texttt{exception\_only}, all four models achieve ${\geq}$99.33\% across all seeds: Qwen-7B (99.83\%$\pm$0.00, 3 seeds), Qwen-1.5B (99.83\%), CodeLlama-13B (99.89\%$\pm$0.10, 3 seeds; seed 123 achieves 100\%), and DeepSeek-6.7B (99.39\%$\pm$0.10, 3 seeds).
47	The cross-model range is 0.50pp (99.39--99.89\%), the tightest of any condition.
48	
49	\paragraph{Exception signals across model scales.}
50	Tier~1 signals are scale-invariant across 1.5B--13B (8.7$\times$): \texttt{exception\_only} spans 0.50pp (99.33--99.89\%) and \texttt{ae\_only} 1.18pp (98.74--99.92\%).
51	CodeLlama-13B seed 123 achieves 100\%, confirming the 2 persistent errors (\texttt{GeneratorExit}; Appendix~\ref{sec:irreducible}) are convergence artifacts; 99.83\% is a floor, not a ceiling.
52	Scale effects concentrate in Tier~2: \texttt{loop\_only} spans 8.57pp (77.06--85.63\%), with CodeLlama-13B below Qwen-1.5B, suggesting architecture matters more than parameter count for lower-density signals.
53	In contrast, \texttt{no\_trace} scales with capacity (72.35--76.47\%, Figure~\ref{fig:cross_scale}) and shows the highest seed variance ($\sigma{=}$4.48pp).
54	
55	\begin{figure}[t]
56	\centering
57	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{fig3_cross_model_gap}
58	\caption{Verdict accuracy vs.\ model scale. \texttt{exception\_only} maintains ${\geq}$99.33\% regardless of scale, while \texttt{no\_trace} shows monotonic sensitivity (+4.12pp). \texttt{nomarker} (markers removed) shows no scale degradation.}
59	\label{fig:cross_scale}
60	\end{figure}
61	
62	\paragraph{VOTA cross-family variation.}
63	Excluding outlier seeds, VOTA achieves ${\geq}$98.57\% across all four models (0.80pp range).
64	DeepSeek seed 42 (84.45\%) is a tokenizer-driven truncation failure (91.7\% of samples exceed 2{,}048 tokens after tokenization; Appendix~\ref{sec:tokenizer_analysis}), demonstrating that VOTA's longer sequences are sensitive to tokenizer efficiency: 1/4 models suffers catastrophic truncation under standard training length. \texttt{exception\_only}'s compact inputs (median 150 tokens) sidestep this vulnerability entirely.
65	
66	\subsection{Component Ablation}
67	\label{sec:ablation}
68	
69	Multi-seed \texttt{ae\_only} ablations use Qwen-7B; \texttt{loop\_only} is evaluated across three models to test Tier~2 generalization (Table~\ref{tab:cross_model}).
70	Table~\ref{tab:ablation} decomposes VOTA on Qwen-2.5-7B and reveals a two-tier hierarchy validated across all four models and three architecture families (Table~\ref{tab:cross_model}): Tier~1 (${\geq}$98.53\%) and Tier~2 (77--86\%) are separated by 12+pp with non-overlapping CIs.
71	
72	\begin{table}[t]
73	\centering
74	\caption{Component ablation on Qwen-2.5-7B ($N{=}1{,}190$). Tier~1 signals achieve near-perfect accuracy; combining them (VOTA) slightly dilutes performance. Entries marked $^\dag$/$^\S$/$^\ddag$ are 3-seed mean$\pm$std; unmarked entries are single seed (seed 42). Per-seed breakdowns in Appendix~\ref{sec:full_results}. Seed $\sigma$ negatively correlates with density (0.00 for exception to 0.52 for loop).}
75	\label{tab:ablation}
76	\small
77	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{%
78	\begin{tabular}{llccc}
79	\toprule
80	\textbf{Condition} & \textbf{Components} & \textbf{Acc (\%)} & \textbf{95\% CI} & \textbf{Tier} \\
81	\midrule
82	ae\_only & assertion-point & 99.64$^\dag$ & [99.26, 99.87] & 1 \\
83	exception\_only & exception events & \textbf{99.83} & [99.58, 100.00] & 1 \\
84	VOTA & exc+ae+loop & 99.05$^\S$ & [98.49, 99.50] & --- \\
85	VOTA\_no\_ae & exc+loop & 99.41 & [98.90, 99.80] & --- \\
86	loop\_only & loop compression & 86.05$^\ddag$ & [83.97, 87.96] & 2 \\
87	full\_trace & complete trace & 81.44{\scriptsize$\pm$1.08} & --- & --- \\
88	no\_trace & none & 75.13 & [72.77, 77.48] & --- \\
89	\midrule
90	Regex (best) & exc.\ type regex & 93.61 & --- & --- \\
91	3-shot (Qwen-7B) & in-context learning & 90.84 & --- & --- \\
92	Zero-shot (4 models) & prompting & 51.0--93.5 & --- & --- \\
93	\bottomrule
94	\end{tabular}%
95	}
96	\end{table}
97	
98	Tier~1 signals are individually sufficient: \texttt{exception\_only} (99.83\%$\pm$0.00) exceeds VOTA (99.05\%$\pm$0.38) by +0.78pp (2/3 seeds $p{<}.05$, all directionally consistent), implying that combining signals introduces noise.
99	Removing assertion points has no effect (\texttt{VOTA\_no\_ae} = 99.41\%, single seed), confirming exception dominance.
100	The Tier~1/2 boundary is unambiguous: the lowest Tier~1 condition (\texttt{ae\_only} on DeepSeek, 98.53\%$\pm$0.30, 2 seeds) exceeds the highest Tier~2 (\texttt{loop\_only} on Qwen-7B, 86.05\%, CI [83.97, 87.96]) by 12.48\,pp with non-overlapping confidence intervals.
101	Tier~2 \texttt{loop\_only} lifts over \texttt{no\_trace} are model-dependent: Qwen shows +10.92pp (7B) and +7.19pp (1.5B, $p{<}.0001$), with the larger 7B gain likely reflecting greater capacity to exploit structured loop patterns; DeepSeek +6.95pp (80.31\%), CodeLlama +0.93pp (77.40\%, n.s.).
102	\paragraph{Rule-based baseline comparison.}
103	The strongest non-trivial regex heuristic (B4: exception class name matching; Table~\ref{tab:regex}) achieves 93.61\% accuracy (F1=0.876), trailing SFT \texttt{exception\_only} (99.83\%, F1=0.997) by 6.22pp (McNemar $p{<}.001$).
104	The gap concentrates on caught-exception boundary cases: regex flags all exception events as failures, while SFT learns to distinguish caught exceptions (benign, inside \texttt{try/except}) from uncaught faults, correctly resolving 6/8 boundary samples (Appendix~\ref{sec:regex_baselines}).
105	Across all formats, SFT outperforms the best applicable regex by +3.62--23.86pp (Appendix~\ref{sec:regex_baselines}).
106	
107	\paragraph{Zero-shot and few-shot LLM baselines.}
108	Zero-shot prompting spans 51.0--93.5\% across four models (42.44pp range); three-shot ICL on Qwen-7B (90.84\%) improves only 1.76pp.
109	SFT compresses this range to 0.50pp (99.39--99.89\%), closing a 6--49pp gap with 4,720 samples (Appendix~\ref{sec:zeroshot_cross_model}).
110	\paragraph{Frontier model crossover.}
111	Two frontier models confirm that SFT's advantage depends on input density.
112	GPT-4o zero-shot achieves 87.98\% on \texttt{exception\_only} and 84.45\% on \texttt{no\_trace}; GPT-4.1 achieves 82.18\% and 77.31\%, respectively.
113	Under \texttt{no\_trace}, both frontier models outperform SFT Qwen-7B (75.13\%) by +9.32pp (GPT-4o) and +2.18pp (GPT-4.1).
114	Under \texttt{exception\_only}, the ranking reverses: SFT Qwen-7B (99.83\%) exceeds GPT-4o by 11.85pp and GPT-4.1 by 17.65pp.
115	This crossover shows that compact diagnostic input unlocks SFT's full advantage, while absent or verbose traces favor general reasoning capacity.
116	
117	\subsection{Format Marker Ablation}
118	\label{sec:nomarker}
119	
120	The \texttt{exception\_only} format includes a \texttt{[No exception data in trace]} marker in all pass-class samples (regex baseline B5 exploits this for 99.33\% accuracy; Appendix~\ref{sec:regex_baselines}).
121	To quantify the marker's contribution, we retrain with all format markers removed (\texttt{nomarker} condition).
122	
123	Qwen-7B 3-seed mean: 97.65\% $\pm$ 0.96 (seeds 42/123/456: 96.55/98.32/98.07\%), a drop of 2.18pp from \texttt{exception\_only} (99.83\%).
124	Qwen-1.5B: 97.82\%, drop of 2.01pp.
125	The marker contribution (${\sim}$2pp) accounts for only ${\sim}$9\% of the total exception lift over \texttt{no\_trace} (24.70pp on 7B).
126	Regex baselines confirm this decomposition: B5 (\texttt{[No exception data]}$\to$pass, 99.33\% with markers) collapses to 28.49\% without markers, confirming it is entirely format-dependent.
127	The strongest semantic regex B4 (exception class matching, 93.61\%) is marker-invariant; SFT \texttt{nomarker} (97.65\%) exceeds it by 4.04pp, isolating the learned semantic contribution beyond pattern matching.
128	
129	\subsection{Preliminary Decomposition of the Full-Trace Gap}
130	\label{sec:density}
131	
132	The 17.61pp gap between \texttt{full\_trace} (81.44\%$\pm$1.08, 3-seed mean) and VOTA (99.05\%, 3-seed mean) can be probed via \texttt{full\_trace\_label\_only} (85.94\%$\pm$1.12, 3 seeds; Table~\ref{tab:density}), which retains the full trace but masks all trace tokens from the loss.
133	
134	\begin{table}[t]
135	\centering
136	\caption{Preliminary decomposition of the full-trace gap (Qwen-7B, 3-seed means). Label-only loss provides an upper-bound estimate of gradient dilution (${\leq}$26\%); the ablation simultaneously changes gradient selection, regularization, and signal distribution.}
137	\label{tab:density}
138	\small
139	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{%
140	\begin{tabular}{lccc}
141	\toprule
142	\textbf{Condition} & \textbf{Acc (\%)} & \textbf{$\Delta$ vs.\ full\_trace} & \textbf{Gap explained} \\
143	\midrule
144	full\_trace & 81.44{\scriptsize$\pm$1.08} & --- & --- \\
145	label\_only & 85.94{\scriptsize$\pm$1.12} & +4.50pp & 26\% \\
146	VOTA & 99.05{\scriptsize$\pm$0.38} & +17.61pp & 100\% \\
147	\bottomrule
148	\end{tabular}%
149	}
150	\end{table}
151	
152	Label-only loss improves accuracy by +4.50pp (3-seed mean), providing an upper-bound estimate that gradient dilution accounts for at most ${\sim}$26\% of the gap.
153	However, this ablation simultaneously changes which tokens receive gradients, removes implicit regularization from trace prediction, and alters the training signal distribution; the +4.50pp may partially reflect these confounds rather than gradient dilution alone.
154	The remaining ${\sim}$74\% (+13.11pp) reflects information overload at inference: the model receives thousands of irrelevant trace lines that compete for attention with the diagnostic signal, regardless of loss design.
155	Cross-model evidence supports this: the tail-truncation sharp performance jump at ${\sim}$500 tokens (\S\ref{sec:trace_length}, all four models) crosses the globals module dump threshold, exposing structured execution markers (\texttt{[Line~X]} variable values).
156	Cross-scale validation on Qwen-1.5B yields a consistent pattern (17\% gradient dilution, 83\% information overload, 3 seeds), confirming that information overload dominates across model scales.
157	CodeLlama-13B and DeepSeek-6.7B both exhibit label-only collapse (28.5\% accuracy, collapsing to the minority class), limiting the decomposition to models whose tokenizers preserve sufficient trace structure under the training sequence length.
158	Error analysis provides direct evidence: all 217 \texttt{full\_trace} errors occur in samples where tokenizer truncation removes diagnostic markers, and among the 61 samples where \texttt{full\_trace} underperforms \texttt{no\_trace}, all exhibit the same truncation pattern, confirming that information overload manifests through a mechanical truncation pathway rather than semantic confusion.
159	The 26\%/74\% attribution is a preliminary upper-bound estimate limited to the Qwen family (2 scales, 3 seeds each); the two factors may interact, and label-only loss changes multiple optimization dynamics simultaneously.
160	
161	\subsection{Additional Analyses}
162	\label{sec:additional}
163	
164	\paragraph{Trace length ablation.}
165	\label{sec:trace_length}
166	Varying the head-truncated trace token budget (256--no limit) on Qwen-7B yields accuracy in a narrow 77.2\%--81.8\% band with a non-monotonic pattern: 1024-token prefixes (77.23\%) underperform 512-token prefixes (80.42\%, $p{=}.002$), because mid-length prefixes retain enough irrelevant content to mislead the model while discarding diagnostic events near the trace terminus (Appendix~\ref{sec:trace_length_full}).
167	This non-monotonicity is specific to head-truncation; under tail-truncation, accuracy increases monotonically for two of three models (Table~\ref{tab:tail_cross_model} in Appendix), with only DeepSeek showing a mild architecture-specific reversal at tail-1024$\to$tail-2048 ($-$1.5pp).
168	
169	\paragraph{Truncation direction and budget curve.}
170	Tail-truncation (retaining the last $k$ tokens) dramatically outperforms head-truncation: among the three models with complete data, tail-2048 exceeds head-2048 by 15.5--22.6pp, with the gap widening at smaller scale (Qwen-1.5B: +22.6pp vs.\ Qwen-7B: +15.5pp; Appendix~\ref{sec:trace_length_full}, Table~\ref{tab:head_vs_tail}).
171	All four models' head-2048 accuracies converge into a narrow 75.3--81.8\% band (6.5pp), comparable to the tail-256 band (72.6--78.9\%, 6.3pp), indicating that signal-poor conditions produce a shared performance floor regardless of scale or architecture.
172	
173	A cross-model budget sweep across four models and three architecture families reveals a sharp performance jump localized between 384 and 512 tail tokens.
174	At tail-384, all models remain near baseline (73.6--80.7\%, only +1.0--4.8pp over tail-256); the steep jump occurs at 384$\to$512 (+13.2--22.0pp), crossing from near-baseline to near-ceiling (${\sim}$94--98\%).
175	This localizes the critical threshold to ${\sim}$400--500 tokens, crossing the globals module dump threshold (${\sim}$384 tokens) and exposing structured execution markers (Table~\ref{tab:tail_cross_model}).
176	CodeLlama tail conditions use label-only loss (\S\ref{sec:density}); cross-model tail comparisons should account for this training difference.
177	Beyond 512 tokens, accuracy converges further: multi-seed validation across all 12 model$\times$seed combinations confirms all tail-512 accuracies fall within a 4.63pp band [93.61\%, 98.24\%] (Table~\ref{tab:tail512_seeds}); at tail-1024, three models reach 96.5--98.2\%, compressing the 6.3pp cross-model spread observed at tail-256.
178	\texttt{tail\_2048} captures 95--98\% of \texttt{exception\_only}'s accuracy across all completed configurations.
179	The residual 2--5pp gap confirms that semantic extraction (identifying exception type and message) still outperforms positional extraction (retaining a fixed window containing non-diagnostic lines).
180	
181	\paragraph{Calibration.}
182	\label{sec:calibration}
183	Calibration mirrors the tier hierarchy: \texttt{exception\_only} achieves ECE = 0.0027 vs.\ 0.171 for \texttt{no\_trace}, a 63$\times$ gap (Figure~\ref{fig:calibration}).
184	This calibration advantage generalizes across architectures and scales: all four models (1.5B--13B) achieve ECE ${<}$ 0.005 under \texttt{exception\_only} (Table~\ref{tab:calibration_model_level} in Appendix), indicating well-calibrated predictions rather than overfit confidence.
185	
186	\paragraph{Error analysis.}
187	\texttt{exception\_only}'s 2 errors have zero overlap with all other conditions' error sets (Jaccard = 0.000; Appendix~\ref{sec:error_overlap}); per-type breakdown confirms all standard exception types achieve 100\% accuracy, with only \texttt{GeneratorExit} (a benign \texttt{BaseException} subclass) causing failures (Appendix~\ref{sec:exception_type}).
188	
189	\paragraph{Length control.}
190	Padding \texttt{exception\_only} to 2{,}048 tokens, even in a semantically loaded position (after the verdict region), yields identical accuracy (99.83\%), confirming the 18+pp gap arises from spurious patterns in verbose trace content, not sequence length.
191	
192	\paragraph{Supplementary OOD analysis.}
193	\label{sec:debugbench}
194	On DebugBench \citep{tian2024debugbench} ($N{=}426$), the condition ranking is preserved: \texttt{exception\_only} 100\%, VOTA 99.77\%, \texttt{no\_trace} 96.71\%.
195	However, narrow margins (3.29pp headroom from \texttt{no\_trace}) limit discriminative power; this supplementary analysis confirms the ranking but cannot establish effect sizes comparable to the primary dataset (Appendix~\ref{sec:debugbench_full}).
196
